Title: Феофана Прокоповича, архиепископа Великаго Новаграда и Великих Лук, Святейшаго Правительствующаго Синода вицепрезидента, а потом первенствующаго члена, Слова и речи поучительныя, похвальныя и поздравительныя, собранныя, и некоторыя вторым тиснением, а другия вновь напечатанныя; части 1-2 (Sermons and Speeches, instructive, laudatory and congratulatory)
Creator: Feofan Prokopovich, Archbishop of Novgorod, vice-president and later first member of the Holy Synod
Publisher: В Санктпетербурге: при Сухопутном шляхетном кадетском корпусе (St Petersburg: Infantry Noble Cadet Corps press)
Publication Date: 1760-1761 (parts 1 and 2 bound together; the title page of part 1 reads 1760)
Rights: Public Domain (published 1760, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Language: rus
Source: Internet Archive item feofanaprokopovichaarhiepiskopavelikagon45 (https://archive.org/details/feofanaprokopovichaarhiepiskopavelikagon45), the item's own feofanaprokopovichaarhiepiskopavelikagon45_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: not named in the item metadata; host metadata date: 1760. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: 63a85e651f932eaa62bd0289a1b171308032e5fa92f455de383d16313ec35673.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Russian online-library scan of a copy with parts 1 and 2 bound together; the OCR reads the eighteenth-century civil type with frequent letter confusions (т read as ш, ѣ as Ь or Б), but the text is continuous.
Content note: Feofan's collected sermons and orations, some reprinted and some printed here for the first time: the editor's preface, then his sermons for church feasts and state occasions, the laudatory and congratulatory orations among them, as the title divides them.
